2.5.1 Test Case 7e.1 TEST CASE HEADER Test case identification DMI Function 7e1.0.1.(7e2.0.1.1.2.0).1.(7e2.0.1.2.1.0).1.(7e2.0.1.3.0.1.0).2 Sound test for unified DMI, no DMI configuration for other STMs, with 1 sound request at once and 1 segment in sound requests.: A sound with 1 segments of 10000ms duration is first played once, then played continuously for 25 seconds and then stopped.
